2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eSchemaCrawler is a free database schema discovery and comprehension tool. SchemaCrawler has a good mix of useful features for data governance. You can \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/schemacrawler-grep.html\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003esearch for database schema objects\\u003c/a\\u003e using regular expressions, and output the schema and data in a readable text format. The output serves for database documentation, and is designed to be \\u003ca href=\\\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diff\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003ediff-ed\\u003c/a\\u003e against other database schemas. SchemaCrawler also generates \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/diagramming.html\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eschema diagrams.\\u003c/a\\u003e You can \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/scripting.html\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eexecute scripts\\u003c/a\\u003e in any standard scripting language against your database. You can find potential schema design issues with \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/lint.html\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003elint\\u003c/a\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eSchemaCrawler can also generate structured database documentation in \\u003ca href=\\\"https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/knowledge-catalog/blob/d44368c15e38e7c92481c5992e4f9b5b421a801d/okf/SPEC.md\\\"\\u003eGoogle OKF format\\u003c/a\\u003e\\nusing \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/scribe.html\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eSchemaCrawler Scribe\\u003c/a\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eSchemaCrawler supports almost any database that has a JDBC driver, but for convenience is \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/database-support.html\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003ebundled with drivers\\u003c/a\\u003e for some commonly used RDBMS systems. SchemaCrawler works with any operating system that supports Java SE 17 or better.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca href=\\\"https://dev.to/sualeh/how-to-get-database-metadata-as-java-pojos-24li\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eSchemaCrawler is also a Java API\\u003c/a\\u003e that makes working with database metadata as easy as working with plain old Java objects.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cblockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThe \\u003ca href=\\\"https://github.com/schemacrawler/SchemaCrawler-Core\\\"\\u003eSchemaCrawler-Core\\u003c/a\\u003e API and tools framework code is available as a separate GitHub href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">SchemaCrawler website</a> for more details.</li>\n<li>Use the <a href=\"https://github.com/schemacrawler/SchemaCrawler-AI-MCP-Server-Usage\">SchemaCrawler AI MCP Server</a> to get deep insights into your database</li>\n<li>Explore the SchemaCrawler command-line with a <a href=\"https://killercoda.com/schemacrawler\" rel=\"nofollow\">live online tutorial</a>.</li>\n</ul>\n</div>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">About</h2><a id=\"user-content-about\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: About\" href=\"#about\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">SchemaCrawler is a free database schema discovery and comprehension tool. SchemaCrawler has a good mix of useful features for data governance. You can <a href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/schemacrawler-grep.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">search for database schema objects</a> using regular expressions, and output the schema and data in a readable text format. The output serves for database documentation, and is designed to be <a href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diff\" rel=\"nofollow\">diff-ed</a> against other database schemas. SchemaCrawler also generates <a href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/diagramming.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">schema diagrams.</a> You can <a href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/scripting.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">execute scripts</a> in any standard scripting language against your database. You can find potential schema design issues with <a href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/lint.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">lint</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">SchemaCrawler can also generate structured database documentation in <a href=\"https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/knowledge-catalog/blob/d44368c15e38e7c92481c5992e4f9b5b421a801d/okf/SPEC.md\">Google OKF format</a>\nusing <a href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/scribe.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">SchemaCrawler Scribe</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">SchemaCrawler supports almost any database that has a JDBC driver, but for convenience is <a href=\"https://www.schemacrawler.com/database-support.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">bundled with drivers</a> for some commonly used RDBMS systems. SchemaCrawler works with any operating system that supports Java SE 17 or better.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a href=\"https://dev.to/sualeh/how-to-get-database-metadata-as-java-pojos-24li\" rel=\"nofollow\">SchemaCrawler is also a Java API</a> that makes working with database metadata as easy as working with plain old Java objects.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The <a href=\"https://github.com/schemacrawler/SchemaCrawler-Core\">SchemaCrawler-Core</a> API and tools framework code is available as a separate GitHub project.</p>\n</blockquote>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Licensing</h2><a id=\"user-content-licensing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Licensing\"
